# MetaTrader 5 SDK Documentation

## Document Navigation

This documentation has been converted from HTML to Markdown format with organized folder structure based on the navigation breadcrumbs.

### How to Navigate

- **🏠 Document Start**: You are here - this is the main index for all documentation
- **📁 Folders**: Each major section is organized into its own folder
- **🔗 Breadcrumbs**: Each page has breadcrumb navigation at the top
- **⬅️➡️ Previous/Next**: Navigate between related pages using the navigation links
